	HalfFloatType,
	NoBlending,

} from 'three';
import { CopyShader } from '../shaders/CopyShader.js';
import { ShaderPass } from './ShaderPass.js';
import { ClearMaskPass, MaskPass } from './MaskPass.js';

/**
 * Used to implement post-processing effects in three.js.
 * The class manages a chain of post-processing passes to produce the final visual result.
 * Post-processing passes are executed in order of their addition/insertion.
 * The last pass is automatically rendered to screen.
 *
 * This module can only be used with {@link WebGLRenderer}.
 *
 * ```js
 * const composer = new EffectComposer( renderer );
 *
 * // adding some passes
 * const renderPass = new RenderPass( scene, camera );
 * composer.addPass( renderPass );
 *
 * const glitchPass = new GlitchPass();
 * composer.addPass( glitchPass );
 *
 * const outputPass = new OutputPass()
 * composer.addPass( outputPass );
 *
 * function animate() {
 *
 * 	composer.render(); // instead of renderer.render()
 *
 * }
 * ```
 *
 * @three_import import { EffectComposer } from 'three/addons/postprocessing/EffectComposer.js';
 */
class EffectComposer {

	/**
	 * Constructs a new effect composer.
	 *
	 * @param {WebGLRenderer} renderer - The renderer.
	 * @param {WebGLRenderTarget} [renderTarget] - This render target and a clone will
	 * be used as the internal read and write buffers. If not given, the composer creates
	 * the buffers automatically.
	 */
	constructor( renderer, renderTarget ) {

		/**
		 * The renderer.
		 *
		 * @type {WebGLRenderer}
		 */
		this.renderer = renderer;

		this._pixelRatio = renderer.getPixelRatio();

		if ( renderTarget === undefined ) {

			const size = renderer.getSize( new Vector2() );
			this._width = size.width;
			this._height = size.height;

			renderTarget = new WebGLRenderTarget( this._width * this._pixelRatio, this._height * this._pixelRatio, { type: HalfFloatType } );
			renderTarget.texture.name = 'EffectComposer.rt1';

		} else {

			this._width = renderTarget.width;
			this._height = renderTarget.height;

		}

		this.renderTarget1 = renderTarget;
		this.renderTarget2 = renderTarget.clone();

import { FXAAShader } from '../shaders/FXAAShader.js';
import { ShaderPass } from './ShaderPass.js';

/**
 * A pass for applying FXAA.
 *
 * ```js
 * const fxaaPass = new FXAAPass();
 * composer.addPass( fxaaPass );
 * ```
 *
 * @augments ShaderPass
 * @three_import import { FXAAPass } from 'three/addons/postprocessing/FXAAPass.js';
 */
class FXAAPass extends ShaderPass {

	/**
	 * Constructs a new FXAA pass.
	 */
	constructor() {

		super( FXAAShader );

	}

	/**
	 * Sets the size of the pass.
	 *
	 * @param {number} width - The width to set.
	 * @param {number} height - The height to set.
	 */
	setSize( width, height ) {

		this.material.uniforms[ 'resolution' ].value.set( 1 / width, 1 / height );

	}

}

export { FXAAPass };

 "content": "// Cel-shaded material set + the code that repaints everything from the palette.\nimport * as THREE from 'three';\n\n/**\n * Hard-stepped ramp used as gradientMap. `stops` is a list of\n * { at: NdotL threshold, v: brightness } sorted ascending; the last matching\n * stop wins, so bands are crisp steps, never gradients.\n */\nfunction toonRamp(stops) {\n\tconst N = 32;\n\tconst data =  "content": "// The tower: procedural ironwork built from tapered trusses, plus platforms,\n// arches, lifts and the summit antenna. usage: scene.mjs preview DIR | scene.mjs check DIR OUT

// scene.mjs MODE DIR [OUT]
//   preview DIR      the agent's check: render DIR, print a summary, save
//                    DIR/.preview/screenshot.png
//   check DIR OUT    the judged render: write OUT/checks.json,
//                    OUT/screenshot.png and OUT/shots/*.png. Reads optional
//                    OUT/input/check.json {modes: [...], mode_param: "tod"}.
// Both serve DIR on loopback with the site's scene policy (CSP, no outside
// requests) and render in Chromium with software WebGL (SwiftShader).
//
// check (version 2) records, per load: console/page errors, outside
// requests, missing files, frame rate, WebGL draw calls and triangles per
// frame, a blank test; plus a second screenshot 3 s later (animation), a
// second fresh load (determinism), one load per mode via ?<mode_param>=,
// and a click on each mode's button (does the scene change?).

  console.error('usage: scene.mjs preview DIR | scene.mjs check DIR OUT');
  process.exit(2);
}
const root = path.resolve(dirArg);
const TYPES = {
  '.html': 'text/html', '.js': 'text/javascript', '.mjs': 'text/javascript',
  '.json': 'application/json', '.css': 'text/css', '.png': 'image/png',
  '.jpg': 'image/jpeg', '.svg': 'image/svg+xml', '.glsl': 'text/plain',
  '.vert': 'text/plain', '.frag': 'text/plain', '.wasm': 'application/wasm',
};
// The policy the public site serves scenes with: same-origin files only.
const CSP = [
  "default-src 'self' data: blob:",
  "script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' 'unsafe-eval' blob:",
  "style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'",
  "img-src 'self' data: blob:",
  "connect-src 'self' data: blob:",
  "form-action 'none'",
  "frame-src 'none'",
].join('; ');

const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
  const url = new URL(req.url, 'http://x');
  let file = path.join(root, decodeURIComponent(url.pathname));
  if (!file.startsWith(root)) { res.writeHead(403).end(); return; }
  if (fs.existsSync(file) && fs.statSync(file).isDirectory()) file = path.join(file, 'index.html');
  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) { res.writeHead(404).end(); return; }
  res.writeHead(200, {
    'Content-Type': TYPES[path.extname(file).toLowerCase()] || 'application/octet-stream',
    'Content-Security-Policy': CSP,
  });
  fs.createReadStream(file).pipe(res);
});
await new Promise((r) => server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', r));
const origin = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;

const indexExists = fs.existsSync(path.join(root, 'index.html'));

// Counts draw calls and primitives per animation frame, page-side.
const GL_PROBE = () => {
  const stats = { frames: 0, draws: 0, prims: 0 };
  window.__wcGL = stats;
  const modeVerts = (m, n) => (m === 4 ? n / 3 : m === 5 || m === 6 ? Math.max(0, n - 2) : m === 1 ? n / 2 : n);
  for (const C of [window.WebGLRenderingContext, window.WebGL2RenderingContext]) {
    if (!C) continue;
    const p = C.prototype;
    for (const [fn, countArg, instArg] of [['drawArrays', 2, -1], ['drawElements', 1, -1], ['drawArraysInstanced', 2, 3], ['drawElementsInstanced', 1, 4]]) {
      const orig = p[fn];
      if (!orig) continue;
      p[fn] = function (...a) {
        stats.draws += 1;
        stats.prims += modeVerts(a[0], a[countArg]) * (instArg >= 0 ? a[instArg] : 1);
        return orig.apply(this, a);
      };
    }
  }
  const raf = window.requestAnimationFrame.bind(window);
  window.requestAnimationFrame = (cb) => raf((t) => { stats.frames += 1; cb(t); });
};

function analyse(png) {
  const img = PNG.sync.read(png);
  let n = 0, sum = 0, sum2 = 0; const colours = new Set();
  for (let i = 0; i < img.data.length; i += 4 * 7) {
    const [r, g, b] = [img.data[i], img.data[i + 1], img.data[i + 2]];
    const y = 0.2126 * r + 0.7152 * g + 0.0722 * b;
    n += 1; sum += y; sum2 += y * y;
    colours.add(((r >> 4) << 8) | ((g >> 4) << 4) | (b >> 4));
  }
  const luma_stddev = Math.round(Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, sum2 / n - (sum / n) ** 2)) * 100) / 100;
  return { luma_stddev, distinct_colours: colours.size, blank: luma_stddev < 2 || colours.size < 4 };
}

// Fraction of sampled pixels that differ noticeably between two shots.
function diff(a, b) {
  const A = PNG.sync.read(a), B = PNG.sync.read(b);
  if (A.width !== B.width || A.height !== B.height) return 1;
  let n = 0, d = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < A.data.length; i += 4 * 5) {
    n += 1;
    if (Math.abs(A.data[i] - B.data[i]) > 16 || Math.abs(A.data[i + 1] - B.data[i + 1]) > 16 || Math.abs(A.data[i + 2] - B.data[i + 2]) > 16) d += 1;
  }
  return Math.round((d / n) * 10000) / 10000;
}

const browser = await chromium.launch({
  args: ['--use-angle=swiftshader', '--enable-unsafe-swiftshader', '--ignore-gpu-blocklist'],
});

// One fresh page load; returns its record and the screenshot.
async function load(query = '', { measure = true } = {}) {
  const r = { query, console_errors: [], console_warnings: [], page_errors: [], outside_requests: [], missing_files: [] };
  const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1280, height: 800 } });
  await page.addInitScript(GL_PROBE);
  await page.route('**/*', (route) => {
    const u = route.request().url();
    if (u.startsWith(origin) || u.startsWith('data:') || u.startsWith('blob:')) return route.continue();
    r.outside_requests.push(u);
    return route.abort();
  });
  page.on('console', (m) => {
    if (m.type() === 'error') r.console_errors.push(m.text().slice(0, 500));
    if (m.type() === 'warning') r.console_warnings.push(m.text().slice(0, 300));
  });
  page.on('pageerror', (e) => r.page_errors.push(String(e).slice(0, 500)));
  page.on('response', (res) => { if (res.status() === 404) r.missing_files.push(res.url().replace(origin, '')); });
  if (indexExists) {
    await page.goto(`${origin}/index.html${query}`, { waitUntil: 'load', timeout: 60000 })
      .catch((e) => r.page_errors.push(`load: ${e.message}`.slice(0, 500)));
    await page.waitForTimeout(SETTLE_MS);
    if (measure) {
      Object.assign(r, await page.evaluate(() => new Promise((resolve) => {
        const s0 = { ...(window.__wcGL || {}) }; const t0 = performance.now();
        const done = () => {
          const s = window.__wcGL || {}; const secs = (performance.now() - t0) / 1000;
          const frames = (s.frames || 0) - (s0.frames || 0);
          resolve({
            fps: Math.round(frames / secs * 10) / 10,
            draw_calls_per_frame: frames ? Math.round(((s.draws || 0) - (s0.draws || 0)) / frames) : null,
            primitives_per_frame: frames ? Math.round(((s.prims || 0) - (s0.prims || 0)) / frames) : null,
            canvases: document.querySelectorAll('canvas').length,
          });
        };
        setTimeout(done, 2000);
      })).catch(() => ({ fps: null })));
    }
  }
  const png = await page.screenshot();
  Object.assign(r, analyse(png));
  return { r, png, page };
}

	function getPMREM( texture ) {

		if ( texture && texture.isTexture && texture.isPMREMTexture !== true ) {

			const mapping = texture.mapping;

			const isEquirectMap = ( mapping === EquirectangularReflectionMapping || mapping === EquirectangularRefractionMapping );
			const isCubeMap = ( mapping === CubeReflectionMapping || mapping === CubeRefractionMapping );

			// equirect/cube map to PMREM conversion

			if ( isEquirectMap || isCubeMap ) {

				let renderTarget = pmremMaps.get( texture );

				const currentPMREMVersion = renderTarget !== undefined ? renderTarget.texture.pmremVersion : 0;

				if ( texture.isRenderTargetTexture && texture.pmremVersion !== currentPMREMVersion ) {

					if ( pmremGenerator === null ) pmremGenerator = new PMREMGenerator( renderer );

					renderTarget = isEquirectMap ? pmremGenerator.fromEquirectangular( texture, renderTarget ) : pmremGenerator.fromCubemap( texture, renderTarget );
					renderTarget.texture.pmremVersion = texture.pmremVersion;

					pmremMaps.set( texture, renderTarget );

					return renderTarget.texture;

				} else {

					if ( renderTarget !== undefined ) {

						return renderTarget.texture;

					} else {

						const image = texture.image;

						if ( ( isEquirectMap && image && image.height > 0 ) || ( isCubeMap && image && isCubeTextureComplete( image ) ) ) {

							if ( pmremGenerator === null ) pmremGenerator = new PMREMGenerator( renderer );

							renderTarget = isEquirectMap ? pmremGenerator.fromEquirectangular( texture ) : pmremGenerator.fromCubemap( texture );
							renderTarget.texture.pmremVersion = texture.pmremVersion;

export class PoolField {
	constructor(max) {
		const geo = new THREE.CircleGeometry(0.5, 20).rotateX(-Math.PI / 2);
		const mat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
			uniforms: { uIntensity: { value: 0 } },
			vertexShader: /* glsl */`
				varying vec2 vUv;
				varying vec3 vTint;
				#ifdef USE_INSTANCING_COLOR
				#endif
				void main() {
					vUv = uv;
					#ifdef USE_INSTANCING_COLOR
						vTint = instanceColor;
					#else
						vTint = vec3( 1.0 );
					#endif
					gl_Position = projectionMatrix * modelViewMatrix * instanceMatrix * vec4( position, 1.0 );
				}`,
			fragmentShader: /* glsl */`
				uniform float uIntensity;
				varying vec2 vUv;
				varying vec3 vTint;
				void main() {
					float d = length( vUv - 0.5 ) * 2.0;
					float a = pow( clamp( 1.0 - d, 0.0, 1.0 ), 2.7 );
					gl_FragColor = vec4( vTint * a * uIntensity, a );
				}`,
			transparent: true,
			blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,
			depthWrite: false,
			polygonOffset: true,
			polygonOffsetUnits: -4,
			polygonOffsetFactor: -4,
			fog: false,
		});
		const mesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(geo, mat, max);
		mesh.frustumCulled = false;
		mesh.userData.noInk = true;
		this.mesh = mesh;
		this.mat = mat;
		const m = new THREE.Matrix4(), q = new THREE.Quaternion(), p = new THREE.Vector3(), s = new THREE.Vector3();
		const c = new THREE.Color();
		this._i = 0;
		this.add = (x, y, z, size, color) => {
			if (this._i >= max) return;
			p.set(x, y, z); s.set(size, 1, size);
			m.compose(p, q, s);
			mesh.setMatrixAt(this._i, m);
			mesh.setColorAt(this._i, c.copy(color));
			this._i++;
			mesh.count = this._i;
		};
	}
}

50:				vec3 col = mix( uHorizon, uMid, smoothstep( 0.0, 0.42, q ) );
51:				col = mix( col, uZenith, smoothstep( 0.34, 1.0, q ) );
52:				col = mix( col, uHorizon, exp( -t * 7.0 ) * uHaze );
53:
54:				// sun / moon
55:				vec3 sd = normalize( uSunDir );
56:				float ang = distance( d, sd );
57:				float disc = 1.0 - smoothstep( uSunSize, uSunSize * 1.05, ang );
58:				float halo = exp( -pow( ang / ( uSunSize * 6.0 ), 2.0 ) ) * 0.5
59:					+ exp( -pow( ang / ( uSunSize * 20.0 ), 2.0 ) ) * 0.22;
60:				col += uSunGlow * halo;
61:				float ring = smoothstep( uSunSize * 1.32, uSunSize * 1.42, ang )
62:					* ( 1.0 - smoothstep( uSunSize * 1.52, uSunSize * 1.64, ang ) );
63:				col = mix( col, uSunCol, max( disc, ring * 0.45 ) );
64:
65:				// craters, only when the light in the sky is the moon
66:				if ( disc > 0.5 && uStars > 0.2 ) {
67:					vec3 t1 = normalize( cross( sd, vec3( 0.0, 0.0, 1.0 ) ) + vec3( 0.002, 0.0, 0.0 ) );
68:					vec3 t2 = cross( t1, sd );
69:					vec2 qq = vec2( dot( d, t1 ), dot( d, t2 ) ) / uSunSize;
70:					float c = smoothstep( 0.34, 0.2, length( qq - vec2( -0.32, 0.3 ) ) )
71:						+ smoothstep( 0.26, 0.14, length( qq - vec2( 0.36, -0.1 ) ) )
72:						+ smoothstep( 0.2, 0.1, length( qq - vec2( -0.05, -0.45 ) ) ) * 0.8
73:						+ smoothstep( 0.16, 0.08, length( qq - vec2( 0.1, 0.52 ) ) ) * 0.7;
74:					col *= 1.0 - clamp( c, 0.0, 1.0 ) * 0.16;
75:				}
76:
77:				// stars
78:				if ( uStars > 0.01 ) {
79:					vec2 sp = vec2( atan( d.z, d.x ) / 6.2831 + 0.5, d.y * 0.5 + 0.5 );
80:					vec2 g = sp * vec2( 260.0, 190.0 );
81:					vec2 cell = floor( g );
82:					vec2 f = fract( g ) - 0.5;
83:					float h = hash21( cell );
84:					float present = step( 0.9865, h );
85:					vec2 off = ( vec2( hash21( cell + 1.3 ), hash21( cell + 7.7 ) ) - 0.5 ) * 0.66;
86:					float dd = length( f - off );
87:					float size = 0.02 + hash21( cell + 3.1 ) * 0.05;
88:					float star = 1.0 - smoothstep( size * 0.25, size, dd );
89:					float tw = 0.55 + 0.45 * sin( uTime * ( 0.7 + h * 2.6 ) + h * 40.0 );
90:					col += vec3( 0.92, 0.95, 1.0 ) * star * tw * present * uStars * smoothstep( 0.0, 0.16, d.y ) * 1.5;
91:				}
92:
93:				// warm haze of the city at the horizon
94:				col += uGlowWarm * exp( -abs( d.y ) * 6.5 ) * uCityGlow;
95:
96:				gl_FragColor = vec4( col, 1.0 );
97:			}`,
98:		side: THREE.BackSide,
99:		depthWrite: false,
100:		fog: false,
101:	});
102:	const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
103:	mesh.renderOrder = -10;
104:	mesh.userData.noInk = true;
105:	mesh.frustumCulled = false;
106:	return { mesh, mat };
107:}

const INK_SHADER = {
	uniforms: {
		tDiffuse: { value: null },
		tInk: { value: null },
		tDepth: { value: null },
		uTexel: { value: new THREE.Vector2(1 / 1024, 1 / 1024) },
		uNear: { value: 6 },
		uFar: { value: 4000 },
		uInkColor: { value: new THREE.Color(0x33241a) },
		uStrength: { value: 0.85 },
		uWidth: { value: 1.2 },
		uDepthT: { value: 0.34 },
		uNormalT: { value: 0.4 },
		uFogNear: { value: 620 },
		uFogFar: { value: 2900 },
		uGrade: { value: new THREE.Color(0xffffff) },
		uGradeAmount: { value: 0 },
		uVignette: { value: 0.3 },
		uSaturate: { value: 1.05 },
		uDither: { value: 0.004 },
		uDebug: { value: 0 },
	},
	vertexShader: /* glsl */`
		varying vec2 vUv;
		void main() {
			vUv = uv;
			gl_Position = projectionMatrix * modelViewMatrix * vec4( position, 1.0 );
		}`,
	fragmentShader: /* glsl */`
		uniform sampler2D tDiffuse, tInk, tDepth;
		uniform vec2 uTexel;
		uniform float uNear, uFar, uStrength, uWidth, uDepthT, uNormalT, uFogNear, uFogFar;
		uniform float uGradeAmount, uVignette, uSaturate, uDither, uDebug;
		uniform vec3 uInkColor, uGrade;
		varying vec2 vUv;

		float invDist(vec2 uv) {
			float z = texture2D( tDepth, uv ).x;
			float d = -( uNear * uFar ) / ( ( uFar - uNear ) * z - uFar );
			return 1.0 / max( d, 1.5 );
		}

		float hash12( vec2 p ) {
			vec3 p3 = fract( vec3( p.xyx ) * 0.1031 );
			p3 += dot( p3, p3.yzx + 33.33 );
			return fract( ( p3.x + p3.y ) * p3.z );
		}

		void main() {
			vec3 col = texture2D( tDiffuse, vUv ).rgb;
			vec2 o = uTexel * uWidth;

			// --- depth creases. Comparing a pixel with the mean of its neighbours in
			// 1/z space is exactly zero on any flat plane at any viewing angle, so the
			// only things that print are real discontinuities.
			float dc = invDist( vUv );
			float dl = invDist( vUv + vec2( o.x, 0.0 ) );
			float dr = invDist( vUv - vec2( o.x, 0.0 ) );
			float dt = invDist( vUv + vec2( 0.0, o.y ) );
			float db = invDist( vUv - vec2( 0.0, o.y ) );
			float cx = abs( dc * 2.0 - dl - dr );
			float cy = abs( dc * 2.0 - dt - db );
			float sx = max( abs( dc - dl ), abs( dc - dr ) ) + 1e-5;
			float sy = max( abs( dc - dt ), abs( dc - db ) ) + 1e-5;
			float curvature = max( cx / sx, cy / sy );
			float depthEdge = smoothstep( uDepthT, uDepthT * 2.1, curvature );

			// --- normal creases
			vec3 nl = texture2D( tInk, vUv + vec2( o.x, 0.0 ) ).xyz;
			vec3 nr = texture2D( tInk, vUv - vec2( o.x, 0.0 ) ).xyz;
			vec3 nt = texture2D( tInk, vUv + vec2( 0.0, o.y ) ).xyz;
			vec3 nb = texture2D( tInk, vUv - vec2( 0.0, o.y ) ).xyz;
			vec3 nd = max( abs( nl - nr ), abs( nt - nb ) );
			float nEdge = smoothstep( uNormalT, uNormalT * 2.0, max( nd.x, max( nd.y, nd.z ) ) );
			float facing = abs( texture2D( tInk, vUv ).z * 2.0 - 1.0 );
			nEdge *= mix( 0.35, 1.0, smoothstep( 0.04, 0.3, facing ) );

			// lines taper away into the haze so the far city stays a wash of colour
			float dist = 1.0 / max( dc, 1e-5 );
			float far = 1.0 - smoothstep( uFogNear * 0.7, uFogFar * 0.9, dist );
			nEdge *= far;
			depthEdge *= mix( 0.35, 1.0, far );

			float ink = clamp( max( depthEdge, nEdge * 0.92 ) * uStrength, 0.0, 1.0 );
			if ( uDebug > 0.5 ) {
				vec3 n = texture2D( tInk, vUv ).rgb;
				float z = texture2D( tDepth, vUv ).x;
				gl_FragColor = vec4( n.r, n.g, z, 1.0 );
				return;
			}
			col = mix( col, uInkColor, ink );

			// grade, vignette, a whisper of dither
			col = mix( col, col * uGrade, uGradeAmount );
			float lum = dot( col, vec3( 0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722 ) );
			col = mix( vec3( lum ), col, uSaturate );
			float r = length( ( vUv - 0.5 ) * vec2( 1.06, 1.0 ) );
			col *= mix( 1.0, smoothstep( 1.05, 0.32, r ), uVignette );
			col += ( hash12( vUv * 1024.0 ) - 0.5 ) * uDither;

			gl_FragColor = vec4( col, 1.0 );
		}`,
};

// Cel-shaded material set + the code that repaints everything from the palette.
import * as THREE from 'three';

/**
 * Hard-stepped ramp used as gradientMap. `stops` is a list of
 * { at: NdotL threshold, v: brightness } sorted ascending; the last matching
 * stop wins, so bands are crisp steps, never gradients.
 */
function toonRamp(stops) {
	const N = 32;
	const data = new Uint8Array(N);
	for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
		const nd = ((i + 0.5) / N) * 2 - 1;
		let v = stops[0].v;
		for (let s = 0; s < stops.length; s++) if (nd >= stops[s].at) v = stops[s].v;
		data[i] = Math.round(Math.min(1, Math.max(0, v)) * 255);
	}
	const tex = new THREE.DataTexture(data, N, 1, THREE.RedFormat);
	tex.minFilter = THREE.NearestFilter;
	tex.magFilter = THREE.NearestFilter;
	tex.generateMipmaps = false;
	tex.needsUpdate = true;
	return tex;
}

const RAMPS = {
	metal: toonRamp([{ at: -1, v: 0.40 }, { at: -0.06, v: 0.56 }, { at: 0.24, v: 0.80 }, { at: 0.60, v: 1.0 }]),
	hard: toonRamp([{ at: -1, v: 0.44 }, { at: -0.02, v: 0.62 }, { at: 0.28, v: 0.84 }, { at: 0.66, v: 1.0 }]),
	soft: toonRamp([{ at: -1, v: 0.54 }, { at: 0.02, v: 0.72 }, { at: 0.34, v: 0.90 }, { at: 0.72, v: 1.0 }]),

export function applyPaletteMaterials(mats, p) {
	for (const k of MAT_KEYS) if (mats[k]) mats[k].color.copy(p.mat[k]);
	mats.cloud.color.copy(p.cloud.light);
	mats.cloud.opacity = p.cloud.opacity;
	mats.cloud.transparent = p.cloud.opacity < 0.999;

	const lamp = p.lights.lamp;
	mats.facade.color.copy(p.mat.wall);
	mats.facade.emissiveIntensity = 0.0 + lamp * 1.35;
	mats.roofed.color.copy(p.mat.roof);

	// Night readability: a gentle self-illumination keeps the ironwork legible
	// in silhouette-free shadow while still banding under the floodlights.
	const lift = 0.06 + p.lights.lamp * 0.20;
	tmpE.copy(p.flood.color).multiplyScalar(p.lights.lamp * 0.05);
	mats.tower.emissive.copy(p.mat.tower).multiplyScalar(lift * 0.9).add(tmpE);
	mats.girder.emissive.copy(mats.tower.emissive).multiplyScalar(0.8);
	mats.deck.emissive.copy(p.mat.deck).multiplyScalar(lift * 0.7);
	mats.trim.emissive.copy(p.mat.trim).multiplyScalar(lift * 0.7);
	mats.stone.emissive.copy(p.mat.stone).multiplyScalar(p.lights.lamp * 0.05);
	mats.glass.emissive.copy(p.mat.glass).multiplyScalar(0.15 + lamp * 0.85);
	mats.beam.uniforms.uOpacity.value = p.lights.beacon * 0.5;
	mats.beam.uniforms.uColor.value.copy(p.tint.grade).lerp(new THREE.Color(0xfff4d8), 0.7);
}

// crop+zoom helper for eyeballing screenshots
import fs from 'node:fs';
import { PNG } from '/opt/weightclass/node_modules/pngjs/lib/png.js';

const [src, x, y, w, h, scale, out] = process.argv.slice(2);
const img = PNG.sync.read(fs.readFileSync(src));
const X = +x, Y = +y, W = +w, H = +h, S = +(scale || 2);
const o = new PNG({ width: W * S, height: H * S });
for (let j = 0; j < H * S; j++) {
	for (let i = 0; i < W * S; i++) {
		const sx = X + Math.floor(i / S), sy = Y + Math.floor(j / S);
		const si = (img.width * sy + sx) << 2, di = (o.width * j + i) << 2;
		o.data[di] = img.data[si]; o.data[di + 1] = img.data[si + 1];
		o.data[di + 2] = img.data[si + 2]; o.data[di + 3] = 255;
	}
}
fs.writeFileSync(out, PNG.sync.write(o));
console.log('wrote', out, o.width, 'x', o.height);

// The rendering pipeline: colour pass + an ink pass built from a depth/normal
// prepass, bloom, grading. All line art comes from geometry, never from models.
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { EffectComposer } from 'three/addons/postprocessing/EffectComposer.js';
import { RenderPass } from 'three/addons/postprocessing/RenderPass.js';
import { ShaderPass } from 'three/addons/postprocessing/ShaderPass.js';
import { OutputPass } from 'three/addons/postprocessing/OutputPass.js';
import { FXAAPass } from 'three/addons/postprocessing/FXAAPass.js';

const INK_SHADER = {
	uniforms: {
		tDiffuse: { value: null },
		tInk: { value: null },
		tDepth: { value: null },
		uTexel: { value: new THREE.Vector2(1 / 1024, 1 / 1024) },
		uNear: { value: 6 },
		uFar: { value: 4000 },
		uInkColor: { value: new THREE.Color(0x33241a) },
		uStrength: { value: 0.85 },
		uWidth: { value: 1.2 },
		uDepthT: { value: 0.34 },
		uNormalT: { value: 0.4 },
		uFogNear: { value: 620 },
		uFogFar: { value: 2900 },
		uGrade: { value: new THREE.Color(0xffffff) },
		uGradeAmount: { value: 0 },
		uVignette: { value: 0.3 },
		uSaturate: { value: 1.05 },
		uDither: { value: 0.004 },
		uDebug: { value: 0 },
	},
	vertexShader: /* glsl */`
		varying vec2 vUv;
		void main() {
			vUv = uv;
			gl_Position = projectionMatrix * modelViewMatrix * vec4( position, 1.0 );
		}`,
	fragmentShader: /* glsl */`
		uniform sampler2D tDiffuse, tInk, tDepth;
		uniform vec2 uTexel;
		uniform float uNear, uFar, uStrength, uWidth, uDepthT, uNormalT, uFogNear, uFogFar;
		uniform float uGradeAmount, uVignette, uSaturate, uDither, uDebug;
		uniform vec3 uInkColor, uGrade;
		varying vec2 vUv;

		float invDist(vec2 uv) {
			float z = texture2D( tDepth, uv ).x;
			float d = -( uNear * uFar ) / ( ( uFar - uNear ) * z - uFar );
			return 1.0 / max( d, 1.5 );
		}

		float hash12( vec2 p ) {
			vec3 p3 = fract( vec3( p.xyx ) * 0.1031 );
			p3 += dot( p3, p3.yzx + 33.33 );
			return fract( ( p3.x + p3.y ) * p3.z );
		}

		void main() {
			vec3 col = texture2D( tDiffuse, vUv ).rgb;
			vec2 o = uTexel * uWidth;

			// --- depth creases. Comparing a pixel with the mean of its neighbours in
			// 1/z space is exactly zero on any flat plane at any viewing angle, so the
			// only things that print are real discontinuities.
			float dc = invDist( vUv );
			float dl = invDist( vUv + vec2( o.x, 0.0 ) );
			float dr = invDist( vUv - vec2( o.x, 0.0 ) );
			float dt = invDist( vUv + vec2( 0.0, o.y ) );
			float db = invDist( vUv - vec2( 0.0, o.y ) );
			float cx = abs( dc * 2.0 - dl - dr );
			float cy = abs( dc * 2.0 - dt - db );
			float sx = max( abs( dc - dl ), abs( dc - dr ) ) + 1e-5;
			float sy = max( abs( dc - dt ), abs( dc - db ) ) + 1e-5;
			float curvature = max( cx / sx, cy / sy );
			float depthEdge = smoothstep( uDepthT, uDepthT * 2.1, curvature );

			// --- normal creases
			vec3 nl = texture2D( tInk, vUv + vec2( o.x, 0.0 ) ).xyz;
			vec3 nr = texture2D( tInk, vUv - vec2( o.x, 0.0 ) ).xyz;
			vec3 nt = texture2D( tInk, vUv + vec2( 0.0, o.y ) ).xyz;

export function createFireworks(count = 560, bursts = 4) {
	const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
	const pos = new Float32Array(count * 3);
	const col = new Float32Array(count * 3);
	const fade = new Float32Array(count);
	const size = new Float32Array(count);
	geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos, 3).setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage));
	geo.setAttribute('aColor', new THREE.BufferAttribute(col, 3).setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage));
	geo.setAttribute('aFade', new THREE.BufferAttribute(fade, 1).setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage));
	geo.setAttribute('aSize', new THREE.BufferAttribute(size, 1));

	const mat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
		uniforms: { uProj: { value: 800 }, uOpacity: { value: 0 } },
		vertexShader: /* glsl */`
			attribute vec3 aColor;
			attribute float aFade, aSize;
			uniform float uProj, uOpacity;
			varying vec3 vCol;
			varying float vA;
			void main() {
				vec4 mv = modelViewMatrix * vec4( position, 1.0 );
				vCol = aColor;
				vA = aFade * uOpacity;
				gl_PointSize = aSize * uProj / max( -mv.z, 1.0 );
				gl_Position = projectionMatrix * mv;
			}`,
		fragmentShader: /* glsl */`
			varying vec3 vCol;
			varying float vA;
			void main() {
				float d = length( gl_PointCoord - 0.5 ) * 2.0;
				float a = pow( clamp( 1.0 - d, 0.0, 1.0 ), 1.6 ) * vA;
				gl_FragColor = vec4( vCol * a * 2.2, a );
			}`,
		transparent: true,
		blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,
		depthWrite: false,
		fog: false,
	});
	const mesh = new THREE.Points(geo, mat);
	geo.boundingSphere = new THREE.Sphere(new THREE.Vector3(0, 400, 0), 4000);
	mesh.frustumCulled = false;
	mesh.userData.noInk = true;

	const per = Math.floor(count / bursts);
	const state = [];
	for (let b = 0; b < bursts; b++) {
		state.push({
			base: b * per, n: per, offset: b * 1.85, period: 7.4, cycle: -999,
			origin: new THREE.Vector3(), dir: [], speed: [], col: new THREE.Color(),
		});
	}
	const PAL = [0xffd35e, 0xff7a6b, 0x8fd0ff, 0xff9ad5, 0xb8f08f, 0xfff2c9];
	function respawn(st) {
		// bursts behind the tower, where the camera can see them
		const a = rng.range(2.5, 5.7), r = rng.range(300, 620);
		st.origin.set(Math.cos(a) * r, rng.range(270, 480), Math.sin(a) * r);
		st.col.set(rng.pick(PAL));
		for (let i = 0; i < st.n; i++) {
			const u = rng.range(-1, 1), th = rng.range(0, Math.PI * 2);
			const s = Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, 1 - u * u));
			st.dir.push(new THREE.Vector3(s * Math.cos(th), u, s * Math.sin(th)));
			st.speed.push(rng.range(26, 62));
			size[st.base + i] = rng.range(1.6, 3.6);
			const c = st.col.clone().lerp(new THREE.Color(0xffffff), rng.range(0, 0.55));
			col[(st.base + i) * 3] = c.r; col[(st.base + i) * 3 + 1] = c.g; col[(st.base + i) * 3 + 2] = c.b;
		}
		geo.attributes.aSize.needsUpdate = true;
		geo.attributes.aColor.needsUpdate = true;
	}
	for (const st of state) respawn(st);

	function update(time) {
		for (const st of state) {
			const local = time + st.offset;
			const cycle = Math.floor(local / st.period);
			if (cycle !== st.cycle) {
				st.cycle = cycle;
				st.dir.length = 0; st.speed.length = 0;
				respawn(st);
			}
			const t = Math.max(local - cycle * st.period, 0);
			const k = (1 - Math.exp(-1.75 * t)) / 1.75;
			const drop = 4.5 * t * t;
			const f = t > 2.0 ? Math.max(0, 1 - (t - 2.0) / 1.6) : 1;
			for (let i = 0; i < st.n; i++) {
				const idx = st.base + i;
				const d = st.dir[i];
				if (!d) continue;
				const sp = st.speed[i];
				pos[idx * 3] = st.origin.x + d.x * sp * k;
				pos[idx * 3 + 1] = st.origin.y + d.y * sp * k - drop + Math.sin(t * 2 + i) * 1.5;
				pos[idx * 3 + 2] = st.origin.z + d.z * sp * k;
				fade[idx] = f * (0.35 + 0.65 * ((i * 7919) % 97) / 97);
			}
		}
		geo.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;
		geo.attributes.aFade.needsUpdate = true;
	}
	return { mesh, mat, update, state };
}

const TARGET = PRESETS[todName];

function setTod(name, instant = false) {
	if (!PRESETS[name]) return;
	if (instant) {
		blendInto(live, PRESETS[name], PRESETS[name], 1);
		targetTod = name;
		todName = name;
		blendT = 1;
	} else if (name !== targetTod) {
		blendFrom = clonePalette(live);
		targetTod = name;
		todName = name;
		blendT = 0;
	}
	syncUi();
	history.replaceState(null, '', `?tod=${name}${HIDE_UI ? '&ui=0' : ''}`);
}
let targetTod = todName;

const tmpV = new THREE.Vector3();
function applyPalette(p) {
	const ex = p.exposure;

	sun.color.copy(p.sun.color);
	sun.intensity = p.sun.intensity * ex;
	tmpV.set(p.sun.x, p.sun.y, p.sun.z).normalize();
	sun.position.copy(tmpV).multiplyScalar(1500);
	sun.target.position.set(0, 130, 0);

	fill.color.copy(p.fill.color);
	fill.intensity = p.fill.intensity * ex;
	tmpV.set(p.fill.x, p.fill.y, p.fill.z).normalize();
	fill.position.copy(tmpV).multiplyScalar(1200);

	amb.color.copy(p.amb.color);
	amb.intensity = p.amb.intensity * ex;
	hemi.color.copy(p.hemi.sky);

export function createPipeline(renderer, scene, camera) {
	const size = new THREE.Vector2();
	renderer.getDrawingBufferSize(size);

	const samples = 0;
	const rt = new THREE.WebGLRenderTarget(size.x, size.y, {
		type: THREE.HalfFloatType,
		samples,
	});
	const composer = new EffectComposer(renderer, rt);
	composer.addPass(new RenderPass(scene, camera));

	const inkPass = new ShaderPass(INK_SHADER);
	composer.addPass(inkPass);

	composer.addPass(new OutputPass());
	const fxaa = new FXAAPass();
	composer.addPass(fxaa);

	// normal + depth prepass for the ink
	const inkRT = new THREE.WebGLRenderTarget(size.x, size.y, {
		depthBuffer: true,
		minFilter: THREE.LinearFilter,
		magFilter: THREE.LinearFilter,
	});
	inkRT.depthTexture = new THREE.DepthTexture(size.x, size.y, THREE.UnsignedIntType);
	const normalMat = new THREE.MeshNormalMaterial();

	const prevLayers = { mask: 3 };
	function renderInk(rendererRef, bg) {
		const prevOverride = scene.overrideMaterial;
		const prevBg = scene.background;
		const prevAuto = rendererRef.shadowMap.autoUpdate;
		scene.overrideMaterial = normalMat;
		scene.background = null;
		rendererRef.shadowMap.autoUpdate = false;
		prevLayers.mask = camera.layers.mask;
		camera.layers.set(0);
		rendererRef.setClearColor(0x8080ff, 1);
		const prevRT = rendererRef.getRenderTarget();
		rendererRef.setRenderTarget(inkRT);
		rendererRef.render(scene, camera);
		rendererRef.setRenderTarget(prevRT);
		camera.layers.mask = prevLayers.mask;
		scene.overrideMaterial = prevOverride;
		scene.background = prevBg;
		rendererRef.shadowMap.autoUpdate = prevAuto;
		rendererRef.setClearColor(bg, 1);
		inkPass.uniforms.tInk.value = inkRT.texture;
		inkPass.uniforms.tDepth.value = inkRT.depthTexture;
	}

	function setSize(w, h, pixelRatio) {
		composer.setSize(w, h);
		composer.setPixelRatio(pixelRatio);
		const db = new THREE.Vector2();
		renderer.getDrawingBufferSize(db);
		inkRT.setSize(db.x, db.y);
		if (inkRT.depthTexture) inkRT.depthTexture.dispose();
		inkRT.depthTexture = new THREE.DepthTexture(db.x, db.y, THREE.UnsignedIntType);
		inkPass.uniforms.uTexel.value.set(1 / db.x, 1 / db.y);
		fxaa.setSize(db.x, db.y);

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// resize
function resize() {
	const w = window.innerWidth, h = window.innerHeight;
	camera.aspect = w / h;
	camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
	renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 1.7));
	renderer.setSize(w, h);
	pipe.setSize(w, h, renderer.getPixelRatio());
	pipe.inkPass.uniforms.uTexel.value.set(
		1 / (w * renderer.getPixelRatio()), 1 / (h * renderer.getPixelRatio())
	);
}

The scene is complete and validated. Final round of fixes:

**Rendering bugs found and fixed**
- **The night sky was washed out** — traced to the sun/moon halo in `src/fx.js`, whose Gaussian was ~39° wide and painted a pale veil over the whole sky. Tightened to a real halo, plus a separate narrow horizon glow (`uGlowW`) and an azimuthal glow pooled at the light's own compass direction (`uSunset`), so dusk/night skies now show their banded gradient.
- **Tree canopies were laced with ink lines** — a low-poly icosphere's per-facet normal jumps tripped the crease detector. Canopies now use a detail-2 sphere with a dedicated two-band ramp (`RAMPS.veg2`), so a round tree reads as one round shape.
- **Water looked like stamped wallpaper** — added value-noise amplitude masking, domain warping and distance damping; 3 cel bands instead of 4, sparser glitter. Waves also run slower.
- **Console was noisy** — `flatShading` isn't supported on `MeshToonMaterial` (silently ignored, 5 warnings), `THREE.Clock` is deprecated, and `PCFSoftShadowMap` was removed in r187. All gone: my own wall-clock timer (still a pure function of time, so loads stay in lock-step), `PCFShadowMap`, no `flatShading`.

**Art direction**
- Dusk is now properly backlit with the sun disc *in frame* (rings and all), a violet front fill to keep the near ironwork from going black, and cool violet shadows instead of olive mud.
- Night: moon placed in the visible sky, brighter stars, darker moonlit clouds, floodlights re-tuned as real floodlights (tight falloff, so the plaza no longer blows out), warmer softer lamp pools.
- Fireworks rebuilt: 5 bursts placed by lateral offset from the default view axis so they always land in frame, double shells, brighter flash-on-burst.
- Tower's pylon above the 2nd platform is now a sparser lattice, closer to the real open ironwork (also ~7k fewer triangles).
- The title card now prints the real beam count it was built with: *2 928 beams of iron*.

**Check results** (`node /opt/weightclass/scene.mjs check /work`): 0 page errors, 0 console errors, **0 warnings**, 0 outside requests, 0 missing files, 13 fps under software rendering, 149 draw calls, all three modes visually distinct (dusk 0.9975 / night 0.9978 vs default), all three UI buttons found and effective, `?tod=` deep links land directly in mode, `?ui=0` verified clean, and two loads still match apart from motion.
